Existing and newly created `sales_customer_owner` roles receive read only; `sales_customer_supervisor` receives read and manage. Engineering supervisors retain quote-level `reference_manage` but do not receive baseline access. Wildcard administrators remain allowed by the established super-admin rule. A one-time IAM grant marker upgrades existing business role templates and authorization revisions.
- Persistence and calculation: `internal_quote_pricing_baselines` stores one revisioned baseline per factory/workshop as validated material and machine rows. Alembic `20260718_0026` follows `0025` and is the single head. Defaults remain the accepted rr2 material and machine constants until a supervisor saves a custom revision. New quote reference snapshots use the current factory/workshop baseline and record `pricing_baseline_revision`; historical quotes keep their frozen snapshots unless an authorized user explicitly syncs the quote reference snapshot.
- Frontend: the permission-aware home action opens a responsive modal. Owners see disabled read-only rows and no save controls; supervisors can add, edit and remove rows, with positive-price and duplicate-key validation plus revision-conflict handling. The UI explains that changes affect new quote snapshots and do not silently rewrite historical quotes.

- Follow-up requirement: business customer owners may adjust the `RMB→HKD` and `HKD→USD` rates displayed in a quote's frozen reference snapshot. The adjustment is quote-scoped: it must not modify the factory/workshop pricing baseline or any other quote.
- Authorization and workflow: the new revision-safe `PUT /api/internal-quotes/{quote_id}/reference-snapshot/fx` endpoint reuses the existing `internal_quote:sales_edit` permission in the `sales-business` scope. Business customer owners, supervisors and wildcard administrators are allowed; engineering-only roles are denied. Values must be positive, no greater than `1000`, and have at most six decimal places. A stale quote-header revision returns `409`.
- Snapshot/recalculation behavior: a real change copies the current frozen snapshot, replaces only `fx.rmb_hkd` and `fx.hkd_usd`, preserves `rmb_usd` and all material/machine parameters, and creates a `manual_fx` reference revision. Every participating populated section is recalculated through the authoritative Decimal formulas. Affected section approvals and any final release/export/handoff are invalidated through the same protected path as reference synchronization; audit action `reference_fx_update` records old/new rates and reference identities. Entering unchanged numeric values creates no revision.
- Frontend: authorized business users see `调整汇率` in the collaboration cost sidebar. The inline editor validates both fields, warns that saving creates a new revision/recalculation and can invalidate approvals, and submits through the API/store with optimistic header revision. Read-only and engineering-only users see the frozen values without editing controls. Successful saving reloads the quote, new reference snapshot, summary and timeline.

- Formula and ownership decision: the frontend previews and the backend authoritative calculator use the rr2 formulas `CUFT = L × W × H / 1728`, `carton HKD = (L + W + 2) × (W + H + 1) × 2 × factor / 1000`, `flat-card HKD = (L + 1) × (W + 1) × 2 / 1000`, and `per-piece packaging HKD = (carton + flat-card total) / pieces per carton`. New Sales payloads own `paper_price_factor`, `product_size_cm`, `color_box_size_cm` and `cartons`; Engineering's carton UI was removed. Historical Engineering carton payloads remain readable and are used only as a fallback when Sales has no cartons, including BuzzBee and Disney P4 conversion, so no migration or historical rewrite is required.

The calculator reads the first Sales carton as the main carton and applies `total cartons = max(round(capacity CUFT ÷ main-carton CUFT), 1)` and `freight + lifting HKD/PCS = route fee ÷ total cartons ÷ pieces per carton` in both the browser preview and the authoritative Decimal calculator.

- Requirement: execute `notification-center-redesign-agent-prompt.md` as a full notification-domain refactor covering the top-bar bell, unified business/system feed, Toast queue, read/handled semantics, resilient polling, sound and restrained enterprise UI without changing authorization or backend workflow boundaries.
- Architecture and state: `TopBar.vue` now only mounts `NotificationCenter`. `useNotificationCenter` normalizes molding-sample and system messages, distinguishes initial/account/panel/manual/visibility/background refresh reasons, establishes silent baselines, uses `Promise.allSettled` with per-source stale-data preservation/retry state, sequence/account guards, optimistic item-level read overlays, bounded persisted IDs plus per-source timestamp/boundary-key observation cursors, a priority queue with three individual Toasts plus a key-backed overflow summary, and Chrome Web Locks around cross-tab notification claims. Same-second baselines larger than the bounded ID cache remain silent while the next same-second item is announced exactly once. Queue and summary entries reconcile against fresh server state, so panel reads remove waiting Toasts and stale completed tasks cannot be downgraded. Clicking a message sends only `已读/read`; actual business actions remain responsible for `已处理/handled`.
- Polling and sound: the center polls every 25 seconds only while authenticated, visible and online; visibility/network recovery refreshes silently, failures back off to 120 seconds, and unmount invalidates pending work and timers. `useNotificationSound` unlocks Web Audio only after a user gesture, plays a low-volume 620/760 Hz two-note cue, applies a three-second local/cross-tab cooldown, persists the sound switch, provides preview, and degrades silently when audio or storage is unavailable.
- UI and boundaries: the Teleported `z-[70]` 430px notification panel and `z-[80]` Toast use the existing Slate/Teal shell, show separate unread and pending counts, all/pending/system filters, explicit handled/result states, source-specific recovery bars, detail/read actions, relative time, sound/refresh/close controls, mobile 12px viewport insets, Escape/focus return and hover/focus timeout pause. The dialog title receives focus on open; tabs use a shared tabpanel, roving tabindex and Arrow/Home/End navigation. Molding notifications retain the existing factory/department/role checks, while every authenticated account may request the backend-filtered system feed so direct-target and internal-quote notifications are no longer dropped.
- Business completion semantics: system and molding notification updates now preserve `handled/已处理` as a terminal state. Internal-quote actionable events are marked handled by the corresponding section submit/review, dependency/reference invalidation, final submit/review, artifact consume/revoke or quote archive transaction; final-release result messages are informational and do not inflate the pending count. Internal-quote notifications cannot be manually forced to `handled`. The production/molding department alias remains same-factory and permission-scoped. No schema, migration, API response shape or permission definition changed.

### 2026-07-18

- Requirement: fix the raw-material-management add dialog where clicking `保存` could not create a new raw material.
- Root cause and implementation: Vue number inputs can place numbers into the form state, but `saveMaterial()` called `.trim()` directly on safety stock and unit price before entering its `try/catch`; a filled value such as `50` therefore threw and appeared as a silent no-op. Empty unit price was also converted to `null` and then incorrectly rejected through a zero fallback. The form now models number inputs as `string | number`, parses optional numeric values safely, validates only non-null values, allows blank optional price/safety stock, and shows validation or API errors inside the modal with an alert instead of only behind the overlay.

- Requirement: make every molding-sample workflow, production-task and system-user timestamp accurate, and raise the notification sound again after the earlier roughly 13 dB increase. Business dates imported from a form or workbook must remain distinct from system submission, update, notification and audit timestamps.
- Root causes: the production page still sent a hard-coded `2026-07-03` value that the backend trusted as the completion date; manual creation converted the user-entered business order date into a fabricated `09:00` system timestamp; full-order edits could overwrite server timing fields; molding backend timestamps depended on an unspecified process timezone; several pages rendered raw or naively sliced timestamp strings; the system-user “earliest submission” summary used the first descending record; and the original Web Audio cue peaked at only `0.018` with a shared decay that made the second tone especially quiet.
- Authoritative time contract: new backend/frontend time helpers standardize the business timezone as `Asia/Shanghai`. Molding creation, update and completion timing is server-owned to second precision; clients no longer send a completion “today” value or overwrite `created_at`, `updated_at` and `completed_date`. Excel/manual `order.date` remains a business date. Existing inaccurate creation/completion values are corrected in API presentation from immutable audit history without rewriting old database rows. System-user and notification responses attach the producing process offset to legacy wall-clock strings while leaving stored authentication/session values unchanged.
- UI behavior: the engineering board and production queue now label business order/delivery/return/due dates separately from workflow submission/completion dates. Full detail, notifications, trial reports, problems and audit trails show Beijing time to seconds. Account requests, password-reset notices and last-login values use the same formatter, and the pending panel computes the true earliest request. Notification ordering uses parsed instants instead of raw string ordering.
- Sound behavior: `useNotificationSound` now uses independent non-overlapping 620/760 Hz gain envelopes with `0.16` and `0.20` peaks. That is about another 7 dB above the prior `0.07/0.085` version and about 19–21 dB above the original `0.018` cue. The switch, preview, three-second local/cross-tab cooldown and suspended `AudioContext` recovery remain intact; non-overlap keeps the peak below about `-14 dBFS` without summing/clipping.

Production Vue type checking and Vite build passed with only the known third-party `@vueuse/core` annotation warnings. The request schema accepts optional department codes only, so direct attempts to remove the three mandatory departments fail before the service mutation. Other departments receive HTTP 403. Removing an active optional section invalidates any final release, resets the section's current effective payload/calculation/review state, advances its revision and the quote header revision, closes its outstanding tasks, invalidates downstream Sales dependencies and writes immutable per-section plus aggregate audit events. Previous revisions remain in history; re-adding the department starts a fresh current task without rewriting history.

A confirmation dialog explains that progress, authoritative cost summary and final release will stop including the section, current/unsaved values will no longer be effective, history is retained and the department can be added again. After confirmation the removed department disappears from the participation rail, the page moves to the first remaining participant and the existing add-participant panel can restore it. Mandatory departments never render the action.

### 2026-07-20 — Preserve Molding material grade after save

- Bug: Engineering mold import can provide only a generic resin family such as `PP`, `1#PP`, `PVC` or `ABS`. Molding then selects a concrete frozen material/grade, but every quote read re-ran the Engineering-to-Molding prefill and unconditionally cleared `grade`; the save succeeded yet the refreshed section again showed `请选择具体料型`, blocked authoritative material pricing and could not be submitted.
- Fix: generic Engineering resin values are now treated as category hints whether they arrive in `material` or `material_type`. A saved Molding material/grade is preserved when it remains in the same family (for example Engineering `PP` with Molding `1#PP + JM350/K8009`). If Engineering truly changes to an incompatible family such as `PVC`, the stale selection is intentionally cleared so an incorrect frozen price cannot be submitted. Exact Engineering material+grade pairs remain Engineering-owned and continue to refresh normally.
- Verification: added regressions for initial generic prefill, same-family saved-grade refresh, incompatible family changes and generic values from either Engineering column. Prefill plus calculator tests passed `18` tests, the complete internal-quote API regression passed `24` tests, the production Vue type-check/Vite build passed, and `git diff --check` reported no whitespace errors (Windows line-ending notices only). No schema migration or data repair is needed; existing saved concrete grades become visible again after the backend uses the corrected read path.

### 2026-07-20 — Real sewing workbook mapping acceptance

- Requirement: re-audit `车缝报价单.xlsx` against its real workbook cells because the imported Sewing detail omitted continuation cutting-part rows and did not reconcile to the source quotation totals.
- Source contract: the current `明细` sheet uses A:K as material, cutting part, supplier, MOQ, low-MOQ charge, usage/yard, unit price, cost, markup, quoted price and remark. A material name applies to following blank-A rows while their cutting part and numeric values remain independent. Product boundaries are the `合计` rows in column I followed by the next product title; a text-only material such as customer-supplied buttons inside a product is a zero-cost detail row, not a new product.
- Fix: Sewing import now forward-fills the material only within its current product, recognizes a total marker in any source column, keeps six actual product groups instead of splitting text-only zero-cost materials into false products, strips an optional `产品：` prefix, and retains the source numeric precision for usage, unit price and markup while keeping at least four display decimals. This prevents the previous four-decimal import rounding from changing authoritative recalculation.
- Acceptance: the supplied workbook now parses `152` detail rows into `6` product groups. The first product contains all `25` source rows (including blank-A 后身/底部 continuations), and recalculates to `42.79559028274 RMB`, matching the source `42.80` displayed total. The source workbook was read only and was not modified.

### 2026-07-20 — Participation removal runtime 404

- Bug: clicking `移除该部门报价` returned HTTP 404 `Not Found` and left the department active even though the source tree already contained the removal API and its authorization/service implementation.
- Root cause and resolution: the process listening on `127.0.0.1:8000` was an older Uvicorn instance whose OpenAPI document did not contain `/{quote_id}/participation/remove`. The stale process was stopped and the current `backend/.venv312` application was started on port 8000. The live OpenAPI document now exposes `participation/remove`; no additional route or frontend code change was required.
- Verification: the focused participation API regression passed, covering Engineering and Sales removal, mandatory-department rejection, unauthorized-department denial, summary exclusion, audit history and re-adding the optional department. Existing browser error banners may remain until the page is refreshed, but subsequent removal requests now reach the backend route.

### 2026-07-20 — Molding material and machine selection persistence follow-up

- Bug: after saving a Molding draft, a concrete material grade could still return to the placeholder when Engineering supplied compatible generic resin values in both `material` and `material_type`. A visually selected machine range could also remain stored as a bare A-code such as `18`, causing authoritative validation to report `未匹配机型价：18` even though the dropdown displayed the matching `18A` option.
- Fix: Engineering generic resin pairs are now treated as one family hint and preserve a compatible saved frozen grade. Molding prefill canonicalizes bare numeric A-codes to their range form (`18` to `18A`), preserves compatible saved machine selections and clears a machine name only when the Engineering machine family truly changes. The editable frontend also writes any uniquely resolved material/grade and machine reference back into the draft payload before save, so the displayed selection and submitted value cannot diverge.
- Verification: the focused backend prefill/calculator suite passed `21` tests; the Molding frontend persistence suite passed `4` tests; the production Vue type-check/Vite build passed. The current backend was restarted on `127.0.0.1:8000`, and its OpenAPI endpoint returned HTTP 200.
- Requirement: Huakang C and Huakang D reuse the same public components as the other physical factories across engineering, PMC/warehouse, production, QA, sales and accounting. Routes and ordinary business records stay scoped to the selected factory, while the raw-material master catalog is one intentionally shared data set for all six physical factories.
- Factory and shared-component contract: `ProductionFactoryContextId` and the production-factory registry now include C/D; only the group context keeps the established Huaxing production fallback. Top-bar switching, sidebar/config entries, department tabs, module cards, generic detail pages, return links, notification targets and direct URL restoration preserve a validated `factory` query. C/D use the shared six-department registry but receive independent empty data objects and empty KPIs/todos until their own factory records exist. Representative files are `src/data/enterpriseMock.ts`, layout/navigation components, module-center/detail views, factory registries and their scope tests.
- Shared raw-material contract: API authorization still evaluates the real requesting factory, but persisted master rows use `factory_id='*'`; a material created from C is immediately readable and editable from D and all other authorized factories. GET/POST/PATCH accept only the six entity factories and reject `group`, `*` and unknown identifiers. The ORM/database enforce `factory_id='*'` and globally unique `material_code`. Files include `backend/app/{api,models,schemas,services}/raw_material.py`, `src/api/rawMaterial.ts`, `src/views/RawMaterialManagementView.vue` and their tests.
- Migration contract: forward-only revision `20260720_0027` groups rows by material code, compares all eight business fields, deterministically retains one equivalent row, preserves single-factory unique records, and aborts before writes if any duplicate code conflicts. PostgreSQL locks `raw_materials` exclusively before inspection; SQLite obtains a write lock before its first read. PostgreSQL online/offline paths are supported, while SQLite offline SQL fails with an explicit online-only error. The migration replaces the old composite uniqueness constraint with global material-code uniqueness plus the shared-factory check. Application startup refuses to seed when legacy non-`*` rows remain, preventing old records from being hidden before migration.
- Warehouse and cross-factory boundary: raw-material master data, including the currently modeled safety-stock field, is shared. Warehouse requisitions, inventory batches and inventory movements remain factory-scoped; the existing demonstration rows are explicitly Huaxing-only, so C/D and other factories never reuse them. Injection schedules, molding orders/tasks, carton-mark records/photos, customer-price artifacts, internal quotes/baselines and notification routes remain factory-specific.
- Async isolation: request generations now prevent C→D→C stale responses across injection scheduling, molding lists/prices/raw-material options, Excel import, manual create/rejected resubmit, trial reports, carton-mark photo/batch/recheck flows, customer-price import/export/artifacts, internal-quote lists/create/clone/baselines and notifications. Switching factory closes or resets stale dialogs/forms and restores only the new factory's draft; stale success/failure responses cannot overwrite current UI or navigate into the wrong factory.
- Verification: complete frontend Vitest passed `70` files / `403` tests with `4` files / `5` expected skips; the production `vue-tsc`/Vite build passed with only the known third-party `@vueuse/core` annotation warnings. The standalone raw-material API adapter assertion passed. Backend raw-material API/startup and Alembic coverage passed `15` tests; Python compile checks and final `git diff --check` passed. Independent frontend and backend reviews reported no remaining P0/P1/P2 findings. Earlier authenticated browser acceptance verified C engineering/production and D production/accounting headings, all six department links, factory-scoped displayed module URLs and a clean console; the later raw-material migration and race fixes were verified by tests/build rather than a new browser pass.
- Local database validation: the stopped local SQLite database received a verified external backup before migration. A standard Alembic `0026` database was therefore rebuilt, all 50 retained tables / 3335 rows were copied by column name and matched by type-aware table hashes, and the two obsolete PIN tables removed by `0003` remain recoverable only from the backup. ### 2026-07-20 — Huakang C/D cross-factory molding dispatch

- Requirement: Huakang C and Huakang D own their Engineering molding orders but have no molding production department. Every non-external C/D order must explicitly assign Huakang A or Huakang B as the production executor; A/B and the other self-producing factories continue to execute their own orders. The origin factory remains the engineering, customer and completion owner, while the executor owns the formal production queue, production actions, material requisition and inventory movement.
- Dispatch and authorization contract: orders now persist origin and executor separately, assignment version/time/operator, and immutable assignment logs. C/D may choose A/B during manual creation or Excel import, and authorized Engineering supervisors/managers may reassign the whole order only before production starts, with a required reason and optimistic version check. Approval and reassignment use conditional state/version updates so concurrent approval cannot notify a stale executor. Engineering reads by origin; production reads and writes by executor; the new `molding_sample:dispatch` permission is factory-scoped and does not weaken existing read-only production roles.
- Production and collaboration behavior: C/D production routes are tracking-only pages and never load an actionable queue. A/B formal queues show both source and executor, support source/executor ID or Chinese-name search, and invalidate a frozen print preview if an order is deleted, reassigned or changes status/version. Actual-material fillback, trial reports, production problems and completion notifications return to the origin Engineering order. Engineering details show the current route, assignment reason/version, immutable history and executor collaboration results.
- Data and migration contract: forward-only Alembic revision `20260720_0028` adds executor ownership to orders, requisitions, inventory batches and movements. Existing self-producing orders backfill to their own factory; C/D non-production history stays unassigned; risky C/D production history or ambiguous inventory linkage aborts instead of guessing. Startup now checks the 0028 schema before ORM `create_all` on SQLite and PostgreSQL, preventing an unmigrated database from being silently polluted. Excel import/export and print output preserve and display origin, executor and assignment metadata.
- Verification: complete frontend Vitest passed `71` files / `430` tests with `4` files / `5` expected skips; production Vue type checking and Vite build passed with only the known third-party `@vueuse/core` annotation warnings. Backend molding API passed `89` tests, Alembic coverage passed `15`, permission/Auth/IAM/system-position coverage passed `79`, and dispatch Excel coverage passed `5`. Python compile checks and `git diff --check` passed. Independent backend and frontend reviews found and closed the startup-schema, approval race, pre-production requisition leakage, internal/external edit, factory-search and stale-print-preview issues; the final review found no remaining blocking issue.
- Local runtime acceptance: a verified backup was created at `.tmp/db-backups/royal_regent_nexus-before-0028-20260720-190342.db`, then the local SQLite database upgraded from `20260720_0027` to `20260720_0028` with all original counts unchanged and integrity `ok`. An authenticated browser scenario created `BP-C-DEMO-001` in Huakang C, approved C→A, reassigned A→B with a reason, confirmed A removal/B receipt, started production in B, saved `1.20 kg` actual material, reported a problem, synchronized one trial report, completed back to C, verified origin/executor search and print-preview ownership fields, then deleted the test order through the UI. Final counts returned to 7 orders / 7 items / 287 raw materials / 26 users, with zero dispatch logs, inventory rows or test-order residue.
- Boundary: implementation and migration acceptance are local only. The local backend remains healthy on `127.0.0.1:8000`; production PostgreSQL migration was covered by generated migration tests but was not exercised against a live PostgreSQL service in this task. The frontend and backend currently share the same tested factory-capability contract, but the frontend keeps a local typed mirror instead of consuming the capability endpoint at runtime, so future factory additions must update both sides. No production database/service, Git commit, push, pull request or deployment was performed.

### 2026-07-21 — Internal-quote dependency invalidation scope fix

- Reported defect: saving/submitting another department could make an already reviewed section show `依赖 revision 已变化` and be automatically returned with `工程分段已更新，请同步依赖并重新核价`, even when only workflow status or unrelated Engineering fields changed.
- Root cause and decision: downstream fingerprints included section workflow `revision/status`, and submit/review/withdraw/NA/reopen actions unconditionally invoked dependency invalidation. Engineering also treated Molding, Painting and Assembly as dependents of the entire Engineering payload even though only Molding consumes projected mold fields. Dependency authority is now calculation-input scoped: Molding fingerprints only the Engineering mold fields actually projected into injection rows; Sales fingerprints valid authoritative section totals; Painting and Assembly no longer declare a false Engineering-revision dependency.
- Workflow behavior: pure submit, approval/rejection, withdrawal, NA request and ordinary reopen no longer invalidate another section. Saving/importing real cost data still compares before/after dependency hashes. Engineering mold projection changes return Molding for resynchronization; unrelated Engineering hardware/auxiliary changes leave Molding and Assembly untouched but invalidate an existing Sales total when authoritative costs change. Approved-not-applicable changes and participation removal continue to invalidate affected downstream totals.
- Files changed for this fix: `backend/app/services/internal_quote.py`, `backend/app/services/internal_quote_artifacts.py`, `backend/tests/test_internal_quote_p2_api.py`, `src/data/internalQuoteDeskConfig.ts`, and `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`. No schema migration is required.

The complete internal-quote backend selection passed `94` tests with `3` expected skips; the earlier run's three setup errors were Windows temp-directory permissions and disappeared with an explicit writable `--basetemp`. Focused frontend layout tests passed `6` tests, production `vue-tsc` plus Vite build passed, and `git diff --check` reported only the repository's existing LF/CRLF notices. The local Uvicorn process was restarted with `backend/.venv312`, and `GET /health` returned `200`.

### 2026-07-21 — Final-release return action fix

- Reported defect: on the summary/final-release page, entering a return reason and clicking `确认退回` appeared to do nothing when the logged-in business supervisor was also the person who submitted the pending final release.
- Root cause and workflow decision: `review_final_release` applied the two-person self-review restriction to both approval and rejection. The request therefore returned HTTP 403, while the frontend displayed the error only near the top of the long summary page. The two-person rule now protects approval only: a submitter still cannot approve their own final release, but, when they also hold final-review permission, may actively return/withdraw it before another supervisor approves. The return remains an immutable `final_reject` review, advances the header revision, restores `ready_for_final_review`, records the reason and permits a later resubmission.
- UI behavior: the return panel now shows `退回中…` while the request is running and renders any failure beside the confirmation controls, so errors are visible in the current viewport. The summary copy explicitly states that the submitter may return the pending release before approval.
- Files changed: `backend/app/services/internal_quote_release.py`, `backend/tests/test_internal_quote_p4_api.py`, `src/components/modules/sales/internal-quote/InternalQuoteSummary.vue`, `src/views/__tests__/internalQuoteDeskLayout.spec.ts`, and `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`. No database migration is required.
- Verification: the targeted final-release boundary tests passed `3` tests; the complete P4 API regression passed `5` tests, including self-approval denial, submitter return, immutable review data and resubmission. Focused frontend API/layout regression passed `2` files / `10` tests, production Vue type-check and Vite build passed, and `git diff --check` reported no whitespace error for the touched files (Windows line-ending notices only). The local backend was restarted with `.venv312`, and `GET /health` returned `200`.

### 2026-07-21 — Legacy final-release export refreshes to the unified desk layout

### 2026-07-21 — Factory-scoped internal-quote customer management

- Requirement and authorization: every physical factory now owns an independent internal-quote customer master list. Users with ordinary internal-quote read access may load the selected factory's options, while only that factory's business supervisor/manager (plus the existing general-manager/administrator authority) receives `internal_quote:customer_manage` and may add, rename or remove customers. Ordinary business users remain read-only and cross-factory read does not grant cross-factory writes.
- Data and migration contract: Alembic revision `20260721_0029` creates `internal_quote_customers` with factory/name uniqueness, normalized matching, optimistic revision control and audit fields. It seeds the five established options for all six factories and backfills distinct historical quote customer names into their own factory. Removing an option never rewrites or deletes historical quotes; historical names remain usable for filtering and cloning.
- UI and API: factory-scoped customer CRUD routes are exposed under `/api/internal-quotes/customers`. The quote-desk filter now uses the current factory's master list plus historical names, authorized supervisors see a `客户资料` management dialog, and new-quote customer selection uses the managed list instead of hardcoded values. Factory changes discard stale customer responses and close/reset customer dialogs.
- Verification and local runtime: focused frontend regression passed `4` files / `31` tests and the production Vue type-check/Vite build passed. Backend customer/API/Auth/system-position/migration regressions passed, including `79` API/Auth tests and `22` migration/system-position tests on a writable Windows basetemp; Python compile checks and `git diff --check` passed. The local SQLite database was backed up to `backend/data/backups/royal_regent_nexus-before-0029-20260721-1520.db`, upgraded from `20260720_0028` to `20260721_0029 (head)`, and the backend restarted healthy on `127.0.0.1:8000`. Seed/backfill produced five options in each factory and nine in Huaxing because four distinct historical values were retained. No production database/service, Git commit, push, PR or deployment was performed.
### 2026-07-20 — Production deployment of PR #126

- Deployment scope: production `main` on `47.115.217.27` was fast-forwarded from `a217340d5196f76e96439649775a61613288ed22` to merged `main` commit `19c2e72518b9ccdfb22716d849b7abd47e45cd80`. The server had not yet received PR `#125`, so this release intentionally deployed both the internal-quote changes from `#125` and the Huakang C/D cross-factory molding dispatch from `#126`. The 74-file live diff required API/Web rebuilds and the forward-only `20260720_0028` database migration.
- Preflight and backup: the clean `main` checkout, healthy DB/API/Web, current `20260720_0027` revision and unpolluted 0028 schema were confirmed before writes. C/D historical production blockers, unsafe requisitions, duplicate inventory identifiers and active non-idle database sessions were all zero; all three inventory tables were empty. Restricted backup `/opt/royal-regent/backups/huakang-cd-molding-dispatch-0028-20260720T115201Z` has mode `700` and contains the PostgreSQL custom-format dump, checksum, 456-entry restore list, deployment/config snapshots, pre-counts, container/volume metadata and pre-deploy permission matrix. The dump is `400560` bytes with SHA-256 `6b31402b7b61ab98458d8d322714b32db90c99e89d996008663c236fe7d698bc`; checksum verification passed. Old API/Web images remain tagged as `rrnexus-api:pre-0028-20260720T115201Z` and `rrnexus-web:pre-0028-20260720T115201Z`.
- Migration and data preservation: after the new images built successfully and the new API image repeated the exact migration preflight, Web/API were stopped for the maintenance window and Alembic upgraded `20260720_0027 -> 20260720_0028`. All 19 pre/post core table counts matched before API startup. All 57 existing A/B/Huadeng/Huaxing orders were backfilled to their own production factory; invalid executor, incorrectly assigned C/D, C/D production-history, null inventory scope and initial dispatch-log counts were all zero. Final business counts remained 70 users, 370 sessions, 43 roles, 182 bindings, 295 raw materials, 57 molding orders, 238 items, 234 molding audit logs, 202 molding notifications and 4 problems; internal-quote and inventory records remained zero.
- IAM and runtime acceptance: startup added only the expected `molding_sample:dispatch` permission (`operate`, `factory_department`, active), increasing permissions from 67 to 68. It was granted only to the general manager, Engineering manager and Engineering supervisor system positions; no unexpected system position received it. Live effective-access verification for Engineering test account `测试账号工程` (`kk`) found one explicit dispatch entry with zero allows: dispatch was denied at all six factories while production-task read remained allowed at all six. The PostgreSQL container ID and volume were preserved; new API/Web and existing DB were healthy with zero restarts. Internal and external `/` and `/health`, Huakang C Engineering and Huakang D production SPA routes returned 200; unauthenticated `/api/auth/me` returned 401. Live OpenAPI contains the factory-capabilities, executor production-task and production-assignment routes, and the deployment-window severe-log scan returned zero matches. No manual business-data or unrelated IAM rewrite, database reset, image prune or volume change was performed.

### 2026-07-21 — System user avatar alignment fix

- Requirement: browser feedback reported that fallback avatars in `/system/users` were visually broken; their initials appeared at the upper-left edge instead of centered in the rounded square.
- Root cause and implementation: the broad scoped selector `.user-cell span` matched the shared `UserAvatar` component root and overrode its centered layout, white color and UI font with username text styles. The user name/account wrapper now has the dedicated `.user-identity` class, and those typography rules target that wrapper only. Shared avatar behavior, dimensions, colors, uploaded images, data loading, permissions and account operations remain unchanged.
- Files changed: `src/views/SystemUserManagementView.vue`, `src/views/__tests__/systemUserManagementView.spec.ts`, `design-qa.md`, and `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`.

Authenticated browser verification checked 12 visible 34 × 34 fallback avatars with effectively zero horizontal/vertical center offset and no console errors. The before/after focused comparison is recorded in `design-qa.md`, whose final result is `passed`.

### 2026-07-21 — Molding sample overview single-sheet print fix

- Requirement: the Engineering molding-sample overview at `/modules/molding-sample` opened Chrome print preview with one actual order followed by two nearly empty sheets. The current short order must print on one portrait A4 sheet instead of wasting paper.
- Root cause and implementation: the hidden print root was nested inside the 1786 px-tall business `<main>`. Print CSS used only `visibility: hidden` for the dashboard and absolutely positioned the order over its first page, so the invisible dashboard still occupied pagination space; the fixed `266mm` minimum order height added a rounding risk. The print root is now a direct `#app` child outside `<main>`. A dedicated body printing state removes all non-print content with `display: none`, keeps the print root in normal document flow with automatic height, injects the scoped A4 portrait page rule, and cleans up the state/style after printing. The fixed page minimum height was removed; distinct selected orders still break between records, while a genuinely long record may continue rather than be clipped.
- Files changed: `src/views/MoldingSampleView.vue`, `src/views/__tests__/moldingSampleRuntime.spec.ts`, `src/views/__tests__/moldingSampleViewLayout.test.ts`, and `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`.
- Verification: both new print-lifecycle and layout-isolation regressions failed against the old implementation and passed after the fix. The focused runtime suite passed `74` tests; complete frontend Vitest passed `71` files / `431` tests with `4` files / `5` expected skips. Production Vue type checking and Vite build passed with only the known third-party `@vueuse/core` annotation warnings. Authenticated local-browser inspection confirmed the hot-reloaded print root is no longer inside `<main>` and is a direct sibling under `#app`; the business page remains 1786 px tall on screen but cannot participate in print pagination. The native Chrome print-preview page counter is outside the browser automation surface, so reopening the real print dialog and confirming `1 张纸` remains the final physical/PDF preview check.
- Boundary: this is a local frontend-only print-layout fix. No backend, API, database, permission, business data, service restart, deployment, Git commit, push or pull request was performed.

### 2026-07-21 — Server-generated molding-sample order identifiers

- Requirement and production context: an online-server create attempt for product number `1016058` failed with “啤办单编号已存在” because the browser derived the hidden primary key as `BP-${product_no}`. Product number is business master data and must be reusable across separate T0/EP/other-stage molding-sample orders; it must not be the unique order identifier.
- Backend contract: ID-less create requests now receive a server-generated `BP-YYYYMMDDHHMMSS-<12 uppercase hex>` order ID. The order header is flushed first so the database primary key is the concurrency authority; generated collisions roll back and retry up to five times, while an explicit legacy duplicate returns the stable 409 response. Generated orders rebuild all item IDs as `${order.id}-NNN`; explicit legacy orders preserve non-empty item IDs and safely generate any missing item IDs. Create DTOs allow omitted IDs, while Edit and item-patch DTOs continue to require them. ID-less direct Excel imports also use the same central generator instead of the previous seconds-only ID.
- Frontend contract: a normal new order no longer derives an ID from `product_no` and omits order/item identifiers from the create JSON. Restored legacy new-order drafts and Excel previews discard hidden source IDs, then adopt the server response ID after creation. Rejected/withdrawn resubmission remains an edit of the original order and retains its existing order/item IDs. The UI now says “提交后生成正式单号”, and an in-flight guard prevents rapid duplicate clicks in one page session.
- Compatibility and data: existing historical IDs are not renumbered and no schema/data migration is required. Explicit legacy create/import IDs remain accepted for compatibility. A regression creates the same product number at T0 and EP and confirms two distinct generated order IDs.
- Files changed: `backend/app/schemas/molding_sample.py`, `backend/app/services/molding_sample.py`, `backend/app/services/molding_sample_excel.py`, `backend/tests/test_molding_sample_api.py`, `src/api/moldingSample.ts`, `src/lib/moldingSampleManualCreate.ts`, `src/lib/__tests__/moldingSampleManualCreate.test.ts`, `src/views/MoldingSampleView.vue`, `src/views/__tests__/moldingSampleRuntime.spec.ts`, `src/views/__tests__/moldingSampleViewLayout.test.ts`, and `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`.
- Verification: backend high-risk regression selection passed `12` tests and the complete molding-sample API file passed `98`; Python compile checks passed. Complete frontend Vitest passed `71` files / `433` tests with `4` files / `5` expected skips; the molding runtime file passed `76` tests. Production Vue type checking and Vite build passed with only the known third-party `@vueuse/core` annotation warnings. `git diff --check` passed with only Windows line-ending notices. Independent final review confirmed the legacy missing-item-ID, direct Excel central-generation, and Edit/Patch typing findings were closed with no remaining blocking issue.
- Boundary and rollout: implementation and verification are local only; the online server, production database and production users were not changed. Because the new Web omits IDs while the old API requires them, deploy API first and Web second. No Git commit, push, pull request, service restart or deployment was performed. Cross-request idempotency remains a separate hardening item: if the server commits successfully but the response is lost, a later retry can still create another valid order; the current guard only prevents duplicate submission within the active page request.

### 2026-07-21 — Internal-quote list deletion for creators and sales supervisors

- Requirement and authorization: the internal-quote summary/list now exposes a row-level delete action only to the quote creator or a business supervisor/manager with the existing factory-scoped `internal_quote:archive` authority. The backend independently enforces quote read scope, exact creator identity or same-factory supervisor authority, and optimistic header revision; cross-user ordinary business accounts and cross-factory operation remain denied.
- Governance boundary: hard deletion is limited to records that have not been finally released, exported or handed off to customer-price conversion. Any quote in `released`/`exported`, with an approved final release, controlled export or handoff record returns HTTP 409 and must be archived so the controlled-artifact audit chain remains intact. Successful draft/workflow deletion writes a durable global Auth audit entry, handles outstanding actionable notifications, and explicitly removes all quote-owned detail, revision, attachment, review, import, export/reference and quote-audit rows so SQLite and PostgreSQL behave consistently.
- UI behavior: eligible rows show a red trash action. Released/exported records retain a disabled action with an explanatory tooltip. A destructive confirmation dialog identifies quote number, product and version, explains that the removal is irreversible, and renders API errors in place. After success the current filtered page and dashboard reload; deleting the final row on a later page steps back one page. The request is made directly through the API adapter with page-local busy state, avoiding stale Pinia hot-update instances such as the reported `quoteStore.deleteQuote is not a function` runtime failure.
- Files changed: `backend/app/api/internal_quote.py`, `backend/app/services/internal_quote.py`, `backend/tests/test_internal_quote_api.py`, `src/api/internalQuote.ts`, `src/components/modules/sales/internal-quote/InternalQuoteHome.vue`, its factory-scope test, `src/stores/internalQuoteDesk.ts`, `src/types/internalQuoteDesk.ts`, and `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`. No schema migration or new permission code is required.
- Verification: the focused delete API test passed and the complete internal-quote API file passed `27` tests. The focused quote-home regression passed `12` tests; complete frontend Vitest passed `71` files / `437` tests with `4` files / `5` expected skips. Python compile checks, production Vue type-check/Vite build and `git diff --check` passed (Windows line-ending notices only). The stale local API process was replaced with the current `.venv312` backend; `/health` returned HTTP 200 and live OpenAPI now exposes GET/DELETE/PATCH on `/api/internal-quotes/{quote_id}`. No database data, deployment, Git commit, push or pull request was performed.

### 2026-07-21 — Molding-sample purpose-first conditional material entry

- Requirement: in the Engineering molding-sample new-order detail grid, `用料用途` must appear immediately before `颜色`. `正式生产` must keep the raw-material database search/selection flow, while `试料` must accept a custom material name without searching the raw-material database. Trial material must continue to preserve entered weight but contribute zero material-balance amount.
- Implementation: the new-order grid now orders `所需用料 → 原料价格(HKD/磅) → 用料用途 → 颜色`. Production rows retain the existing combobox, enabled-material validation, price lookup and composition flow. Trial rows render a 255-character custom textbox and do not expose the raw-material list or validate names against enabled database materials; custom single-material input is synchronized to one 100% virgin component so submit/restoration cannot overwrite it with stale composition data. Switching an unknown custom trial material back to production clears it and requires a valid database selection. Existing multi-material trial compositions remain editable for Excel/backward compatibility.
- Excel mapping: the Engineering import template and formal item export now order `所需用料 / 原料 → 用料用途 → 颜色`; the usage dropdown moved to `D9:D38` and mold status to `M9:M38`. The guide tells users to enter an enabled database material name for formal production and allows arbitrary trial text. Current and historical workbooks remain compatible because import maps by header rather than fixed position. Valid multi-material text is still normalized; malformed production composition remains rejected, while arbitrary trial text, including percentage-like text, is preserved as one 100% component.
- Compatibility: backend create/edit schemas already accept arbitrary material names and component payloads without a raw-material foreign key, so no schema or migration change was required. Existing balance behavior is unchanged: trial weight remains available, while the material-balance monetary contribution remains zero. Excel intentionally does not query or enforce live raw-material membership; the browser production picker remains the database-enforced selection surface, and the workbook guide states the rule without embedding a stale material snapshot.
- Files changed: `src/views/MoldingSampleView.vue`, `src/views/__tests__/moldingSampleRuntime.spec.ts`, `src/views/__tests__/moldingSampleViewLayout.test.ts`, `backend/app/services/molding_sample_excel.py`, `backend/tests/test_molding_sample_api.py`, `design-qa.md`, `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`, and the browser/workbook QA artifacts under `outputs/molding-sample-material-purpose-20260721/`.
- Verification: focused layout/manual-create/runtime regressions passed; complete frontend Vitest passed `71` files with `434` tests passed and `5` expected skips; production Vue type checking and Vite build passed with only the known third-party `@vueuse/core` annotation warnings. Fourteen focused backend Excel/API tests passed, then the complete molding-sample backend file passed `99` tests; coverage includes formal and trial round-trip, current and legacy header order, template validation coordinates, percentage-like trial text, malformed production composition, and dispatch compatibility. Authenticated browser verification confirmed the new header/row order, the production combobox, the trial ordinary textbox with a custom value and no listbox, restoration to the blank production state, and zero browser console warnings/errors. The generated two-sheet workbook was inspected and rendered at `A1:N15` / `A1:D30`; both sheets were legible and the formula-error scan found zero matches. Independent final review found no blocking or high-confidence issue.
- Visual QA boundary: both production and trial browser screenshots were saved at the same `1280 × 556` viewport and inspected, and both spreadsheet sheets passed structural and visual checks. The original annotated browser-comment images were not exposed as local files, so the required single-input browser side-by-side composite could not be produced; `design-qa.md` records the strict visual comparison result as `blocked` even though direct browser, spreadsheet and functional acceptance passed.

### 2026-07-21 — Shared natural-pagination molding-sample print documents

- Requirement and superseding decision: the Engineering notice and Production task printouts now follow the supplied portrait and landscape A4 mockups. This change supersedes the earlier fixed-`273mm`, one-order/one-sheet compression rule: short records still fit naturally, while long records continue onto additional sheets without shrinking below 9.5 pt or clipping mold rows.
- Shared rendering contract: `MoldingSampleEngineeringPrintDocument.vue` and `MoldingSampleProductionPrintDocument.vue` are the single sources for both the on-screen preview and direct-child hidden print roots. Engineering keeps the brand/title/order block, status band, ordered 4 × 2 metadata grid, reason/rejection information and authorized six-column mold/cost table. Production keeps only the title/order/stage, reason and six-column Engineering mold table; return-mold, routing/ownership, dispatch time, full metadata, production fillback and cost content are excluded.
- Print behavior: Engineering injects `A4 portrait` with 8 mm page margins; Production injects `A4 landscape` with 6 mm margins. Shared CSS repeats table headers and order context, avoids splitting individual mold rows, uses normal document flow and breaks only between distinct orders. Product/customer precedes product number, and time labels omit the Beijing-time suffix without changing stored timestamp semantics.
- Files changed: `src/components/molding/print/MoldingSampleEngineeringPrintDocument.vue`, `src/components/molding/print/MoldingSampleProductionPrintDocument.vue`, `src/components/molding/print/moldingSamplePrint.css`, `src/components/molding/print/__tests__/moldingSamplePrintDocuments.spec.ts`, both molding-sample views, their focused layout/runtime tests, `design-qa.md`, `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`, and the QA artifacts under `outputs/molding-sample-print-optimization-20260721/`.
- Verification: shared component coverage passed with 1/4/8/12 mold rows; focused print/runtime coverage passed 85 tests. Complete frontend Vitest passed 72 files with 446 tests passed and 5 expected skips; TypeScript project checking and the Vite production build passed. Authenticated in-app browser inspection opened both real print previews, verified required/removed content in accessibility snapshots, and compared reference images with implementation screenshots in combined evidence. `design-qa.md` records the final visual result as `passed`.
- Boundary: this is a local frontend-only print presentation change. The operating-system printer dialog and physical printer margins depend on local browser/printer configuration and were not captured. No backend, API, database, schema, permission, business data, deployment, Git commit, push or pull request was performed.
